 - that N^{-1/4} is a MATCHING local minimax rate with BOTH an upper and a lower bound, giving

   identifiable;
 - that the N^{-1/4} rate arises from root-N estimation of the squared separation composed
   with the singular square-root map, and that the earlier N^{-1/3} from the stopped

 - the replaced machinery: sampled pole-order nondegeneracy in place of covariance

rather than reaching for generality he had not costed.

used as evidence, and note his own earlier warning that for infinite-sample theorems numerics
can never substitute for a uniform remainder argument.

## Instruction Priority

Superpowers skills override default system prompt behavior, but **user instructions always take precedence**:

1. **User's explicit instructions** (CLAUDE.md, GEMINI.md, AGENTS.md, direct requests) â€” highest priority
2. **Superpowers skills** â€” override default system behavior where they conflict
3. **Default system prompt** â€” lowest priority

If CLAUDE.md, GEMINI.md, or AGENTS.md says "don't use TDD" and a skill says "always use TDD," follow the user's instructions. The user is in control.

## How to Access Skills

**In Claude Code:** Use the `Skill` tool. When you invoke a skill, its content is loaded and presented to youâ€”follow it directly. Never use the Read tool on skill files.

**In Copilot CLI:** Use the `skill` tool. Skills are auto-discovered from installed plugins. The `skill` tool works the same as Claude Code's `Skill` tool.

**In Gemini CLI:** Skills activate via the `activate_skill` tool. Gemini loads skill metadata at session start and activates the full content on demand.

**In other environments:** Check your platform's documentation for how skills are loaded.

## Platform Adaptation

Skills use Claude Code tool names. Non-CC platforms: see `references/copilot-tools.md` (Copilot CLI), `references/codex-tools.md` (Codex) for tool equivalents. Gemini CLI users get the tool mapping loaded automatically via GEMINI.md.

# Using Skills

## The Rule

**Invoke relevant or requested skills BEFORE any response or action.** Even a 1% chance a skill might apply means that you should invoke the skill to check. If an invoked skill turns out to be wrong for the situation, you don't need to use it.

```dot
digraph skill_flow {
    "User message received" [shape=doublecircle];
    "About to EnterPlanMode?" [shape=doublecircle];
    "Already brainstormed?" [shape=diamond];
    "Invoke brainstorming skill" [shape=box];
    "Might any skill apply?" [shape=diamond];
    "Invoke Skill tool" [shape=box];
    "Announce: 'Using [skill] to [purpose]'" [shape=box];
    "Has checklist?" [shape=diamond];
    "Create TodoWrite todo per item" [shape=box];
    "Follow skill exactly" [shape=box];

    "About to EnterPlanMode?" -> "Already brainstormed?";
    "Already brainstormed?" -> "Invoke brainstorming skill" [label="no"];
    "Already brainstormed?" -> "Might any skill apply?" [label="yes"];
    "Invoke brainstorming skill" -> "Might any skill apply?";

    "User message received" -> "Might any skill apply?";
    "Might any skill apply?" -> "Invoke Skill tool" [label="yes, even 1%"];
    "Might any skill apply?" -> "Respond (including clarifications)" [label="definitely not"];

}
```

## Red Flags

These thoughts mean STOPâ€”you're rationalizing:

| Thought | Reality |
|---------|---------|
| "This is just a simple question" | Questions are tasks. Check for skills. |
| "I need more context first" | Skill check comes BEFORE clarifying questions. |
| "Let me explore the codebase first" | Skills tell you HOW to explore. Check first. |
| "I can check git/files quickly" | Files lack conversation context. Check for skills. |
| "Let me gather information first" | Skills tell you HOW to gather information. |
| "This doesn't need a formal skill" | If a skill exists, use it. |
| "I remember this skill" | Skills evolve. Read current version. |
| "This doesn't count as a task" | Action = task. Check for skills. |
| "The skill is overkill" | Simple things become complex. Use it. |
| "I'll just do this one thing first" | Check BEFORE doing anything. |
| "This feels productive" | Undisciplined action wastes time. Skills prevent this. |
| "I know what that means" | Knowing the concept â‰  using the skill. Invoke it. |

## Skill Priority

When multiple skills could apply, use this order:

1. **Process skills first** (brainstorming, debugging) - these determine HOW to approach the task
2. **Implementation skills second** (frontend-design, mcp-builder) - these guide execution

"Let's build X" â†’ brainstorming first, then implementation skills.
"Fix this bug" â†’ debugging first, then domain-specific skills.

## Skill Types

**Rigid** (TDD, debugging): Follow exactly. Don't adapt away discipline.

**Flexible** (patterns): Adapt principles to context.

The skill itself tells you which.

## User Instructions

Instructions say WHAT, not HOW. "Add X" or "Fix Y" doesn't mean skip workflows.

# Brainstorming Ideas Into Designs

Help turn ideas into fully formed designs and specs through natural collaborative dialogue.

Start by understanding the current project context, then ask questions one at a time to refine the idea. Once you understand what you're building, present the design and get user approval.

<HARD-GATE>
Do NOT invoke any implementation skill, write any code, scaffold any project, or take any implementation action until you have presented a design and the user has approved it. This applies to EVERY project regardless of perceived simplicity.
</HARD-GATE>

## Anti-Pattern: "This Is Too Simple To Need A Design"

Every project goes through this process. A todo list, a single-function utility, a config change â€” all of them. "Simple" projects are where unexamined assumptions cause the most wasted work. The design can be short (a few sentences for truly simple projects), but you MUST present it and get approval.

## Checklist

You MUST create a task for each of these items and complete them in order:

1. **Explore project context** â€” check files, docs, recent commits
2. **Offer visual companion** (if topic will involve visual questions) â€” this is its own message, not combined with a clarifying question. See the Visual Companion section below.
3. **Ask clarifying questions** â€” one at a time, understand purpose/constraints/success criteria
4. **Propose 2-3 approaches** â€” with trade-offs and your recommendation
5. **Present design** â€” in sections scaled to their complexity, get user approval after each section
6. **Write design doc** â€” save to `docs/superpowers/specs/YYYY-MM-DD-<topic>-design.md` and commit
7. **Spec self-review** â€” quick inline check for placeholders, contradictions, ambiguity, scope (see below)
8. **User reviews written spec** â€” ask user to review the spec file before proceeding
9. **Transition to implementation** â€” invoke writing-plans skill to create implementation plan

## Process Flow

```dot
digraph brainstorming {
    "Explore project context" [shape=box];
    "Visual questions ahead?" [shape=diamond];
    "Offer Visual Companion\n(own message, no other content)" [shape=box];
    "Ask clarifying questions" [shape=box];
    "Propose 2-3 approaches" [shape=box];
    "Present design sections" [shape=box];
    "User approves design?" [shape=diamond];
    "Write design doc" [shape=box];
    "Spec self-review\n(fix inline)" [shape=box];
    "User reviews spec?" [shape=diamond];
    "Invoke writing-plans skill" [shape=doublecircle];

    "Explore project context" -> "Visual questions ahead?";
    "Visual questions ahead?" -> "Offer Visual Companion\n(own message, no other content)" [label="yes"];
    "Visual questions ahead?" -> "Ask clarifying questions" [label="no"];
    "Offer Visual Companion\n(own message, no other content)" -> "Ask clarifying questions";
    "Ask clarifying questions" -> "Propose 2-3 approaches";
    "Propose 2-3 approaches" -> "Present design sections";
    "Present design sections" -> "User approves design?";
    "User approves design?" -> "Present design sections" [label="no, revise"];
    "User approves design?" -> "Write design doc" [label="yes"];
    "Write design doc" -> "Spec self-review\n(fix inline)";
    "Spec self-review\n(fix inline)" -> "User reviews spec?";
    "User reviews spec?" -> "Write design doc" [label="changes requested"];
    "User reviews spec?" -> "Invoke writing-plans skill" [label="approved"];
}
```

**The terminal state is invoking writing-plans.** Do NOT invoke frontend-design, mcp-builder, or any other implementation skill. The ONLY skill you invoke after brainstorming is writing-plans.

## The Process

**Understanding the idea:**

- Check out the current project state first (files, docs, recent commits)
- Before asking detailed questions, assess scope: if the request describes multiple independent subsystems (e.g., "build a platform with chat, file storage, billing, and analytics"), flag this immediately. Don't spend questions refining details of a project that needs to be decomposed first.
- If the project is too large for a single spec, help the user decompose into sub-projects: what are the independent pieces, how do they relate, what order should they be built? Then brainstorm the first sub-project through the normal design flow. Each sub-project gets its own spec â†’ plan â†’ implementation cycle.
- For appropriately-scoped projects, ask questions one at a time to refine the idea
- Prefer multiple choice questions when possible, but open-ended is fine too
- Only one question per message - if a topic needs more exploration, break it into multiple questions
- Focus on understanding: purpose, constraints, success criteria

**Exploring approaches:**

- Propose 2-3 different approaches with trade-offs
- Present options conversationally with your recommendation and reasoning
- Lead with your recommended option and explain why

**Presenting the design:**

- Once you believe you understand what you're building, present the design
- Scale each section to its complexity: a few sentences if straightforward, up to 200-300 words if nuanced
- Ask after each section whether it looks right so far
- Cover: architecture, components, data flow, error handling, testing
- Be ready to go back and clarify if something doesn't make sense

**Design for isolation and clarity:**

- Break the system into smaller units that each have one clear purpose, communicate through well-defined interfaces, and can be understood and tested independently
- For each unit, you should be able to answer: what does it do, how do you use it, and what does it depend on?
- Can someone understand what a unit does without reading its internals? Can you change the internals without breaking consumers? If not, the boundaries need work.
- Smaller, well-bounded units are also easier for you to work with - you reason better about code you can hold in context at once, and your edits are more reliable when files are focused. When a file grows large, that's often a signal that it's doing too much.

**Working in existing codebases:**

- Explore the current structure before proposing changes. Follow existing patterns.
- Where existing code has problems that affect the work (e.g., a file that's grown too large, unclear boundaries, tangled responsibilities), include targeted improvements as part of the design - the way a good developer improves code they're working in.
- Don't propose unrelated refactoring. Stay focused on what serves the current goal.

## After the Design

**Documentation:**

- Write the validated design (spec) to `docs/superpowers/specs/YYYY-MM-DD-<topic>-design.md`
  - (User preferences for spec location override this default)
- Use elements-of-style:writing-clearly-and-concisely skill if available
- Commit the design document to git

**Spec Self-Review:**
After writing the spec document, look at it with fresh eyes:

1. **Placeholder scan:** Any "TBD", "TODO", incomplete sections, or vague requirements? Fix them.
2. **Internal consistency:** Do any sections contradict each other? Does the architecture match the feature descriptions?
3. **Scope check:** Is this focused enough for a single implementation plan, or does it need decomposition?
4. **Ambiguity check:** Could any requirement be interpreted two different ways? If so, pick one and make it explicit.

Fix any issues inline. No need to re-review â€” just fix and move on.

**User Review Gate:**
After the spec review loop passes, ask the user to review the written spec before proceeding:

> "Spec written and committed to `<path>`. Please review it and let me know if you want to make any changes before we start writing out the implementation plan."

Wait for the user's response. If they request changes, make them and re-run the spec review loop. Only proceed once the user approves.

**Implementation:**

- Invoke the writing-plans skill to create a detailed implementation plan
- Do NOT invoke any other skill. writing-plans is the next step.

## Key Principles

- **One question at a time** - Don't overwhelm with multiple questions
- **Multiple choice preferred** - Easier to answer than open-ended when possible
- **YAGNI ruthlessly** - Remove unnecessary features from all designs
- **Explore alternatives** - Always propose 2-3 approaches before settling
- **Incremental validation** - Present design, get approval before moving on
- **Be flexible** - Go back and clarify when something doesn't make sense

## Visual Companion

A browser-based companion for showing mockups, diagrams, and visual options during brainstorming. Available as a tool â€” not a mode. Accepting the companion means it's available for questions that benefit from visual treatment; it does NOT mean every question goes through the browser.

**Offering the companion:** When you anticipate that upcoming questions will involve visual content (mockups, layouts, diagrams), offer it once for consent:
> "Some of what we're working on might be easier to explain if I can show it to you in a web browser. I can put together mockups, diagrams, comparisons, and other visuals as we go. This feature is still new and can be token-intensive. Want to try it? (Requires opening a local URL)"

**This offer MUST be its own message.** Do not combine it with clarifying questions, context summaries, or any other content. The message should contain ONLY the offer above and nothing else. Wait for the user's response before continuing. If they decline, proceed with text-only brainstorming.

**Per-question decision:** Even after the user accepts, decide FOR EACH QUESTION whether to use the browser or the terminal. The test: **would the user understand this better by seeing it than reading it?**

- **Use the browser** for content that IS visual â€” mockups, wireframes, layout comparisons, architecture diagrams, side-by-side visual designs
- **Use the terminal** for content that is text â€” requirements questions, conceptual choices, tradeoff lists, A/B/C/D text options, scope decisions

A question about a UI topic is not automatically a visual question. "What does personality mean in this context?" is a conceptual question â€” use the terminal. "Which wizard layout works better?" is a visual question â€” use the browser.

If they agree to the companion, read the detailed guide before proceeding:
`skills/brainstorming/visual-companion.md`

\section{Stationary collision experiments for sampled serial phase-type renewals}
\label{sec:stationary-serial-collision-main}

This section changes the inferential object from a deterministic product of
complete Palm gaps to the stationary binary record itself.  We first close
that gap for the present two-state sampled counter and then treat one isolated
double collision in an arbitrary fixed serial order.  Throughout this section
the sampling interval \(\Delta\tau>0\) is known.

For two finite multisets \(A=\{\!\{a_1,\ldots,a_n\}\!\}\) and
\(B=\{\!\{b_1,\ldots,b_n\}\!\}\), write
\[
 d_{\rm m}(A,B)=\min_{\pi\in\mathfrak S_n}
       \max_{1\le i\le n}|a_i-b_{\pi(i)}|
\tag{SC1}
\]
for optimal-matching distance.  This metric retains multiplicities.

\begin{paperthm}[Stationary two-state exchange experiment and sharp recovery]
\label{thm:stationary-two-state-collision-main}
Fix \(0<\varepsilon<M<\infty\).  In the sampled-counter model of
\Cref{ass:model-inference-contract-main}, use the quotient coordinates
\[
 c=\frac{\Gamma+\kappa_{\mathrm r}}2,
 \qquad d=\frac{\Gamma-\kappa_{\mathrm r}}2,
 \qquad \delta=d^2\ge0.
\tag{SC2}
\]
Let \(P_{c,\delta}^{(N)}\) be the law of the stationary binary record
\((A_0,\ldots,A_{N-1})\), and let \(g_{c,\delta}\), \(S_{c,\delta}\), and
\(\mu_{c,\delta}=\sum_{k\ge0}S_{c,\delta}(k)\) denote its Palm gap mass,
tail, and mean cycle length.  At \(\delta=0\), set
\[
 \dot\ell_c(k)=\partial_c\log g_{c,0}(k),\qquad
 \dot\ell_\delta(k)=\partial_\delta\log g_{c,\delta}(k)|_{\delta=0},
\]
and define the calendar information
\[
 I_2(c)=\frac1{\mu_{c,0}}
 \sum_{k\ge0}g_{c,0}(k)
 \begin{pmatrix}\dot\ell_c(k)\\ \dot\ell_\delta(k)\end{pmatrix}
 \begin{pmatrix}\dot\ell_c(k)&\dot\ell_\delta(k)\end{pmatrix}.
\tag{SC3}
\]
Then the following conclusions hold uniformly for \(c\in[\varepsilon,M]\).

\begin{enumerate}[label=\textup{(\roman*)},leftmargin=2.6em]
\item If \(u\in\RR\) and \(v\in[0,\infty)\) range over bounded sets, then
\[
 \log\frac{dP_{c+u/\sqrt N,v/\sqrt N}^{(N)}}
               {dP_{c,0}^{(N)}}
 =\begin{pmatrix}u\\v\end{pmatrix}^{\!\top}\Delta_{N,c}
 -\frac12\begin{pmatrix}u\\v\end{pmatrix}^{\!\top}
 I_2(c)\begin{pmatrix}u\\v\end{pmatrix}+o_{P_{c,0}}(1),
\tag{SC4}
\]
where the remainder is uniform in \((c,u,v)\) and
\(\Delta_{N,c}\Rightarrow\mathcal N_2\{0,I_2(c)\}\) uniformly in bounded
Lipschitz distance.  Thus the stationary-record experiments converge to the
Gaussian shift on the half-plane \(\RR\times[0,\infty)\).

\item The continuous survival transform has a double pole at the collision,
whereas its collision derivative has an exact fourth-order pole:
\[
 \mathcal L\{\overline F_{c,\delta}\}(s)
 =\frac1s\left\{1-
 \frac{c^2-\delta}{(s+c)^2-\delta}\right\},
\quad
 \partial_\delta\mathcal L\{\overline F_{c,\delta}\}(s)|_0
 =\frac{s+2c}{(s+c)^4}.
\tag{SC5}
\]
The centre derivative is \(-2c/(s+c)^3\).  Consequently the sampled
collision derivative contains a nonzero
\(k^3e^{-c\Delta\tau k}\) term, while the centre derivative has degree at
most two at that exponential base.  Hence
\[
 \inf_{c\in[\varepsilon,M]}\lambda_{\min}\{I_2(c)\}>0.
\tag{SC6}
\]

\item Partition \(I_2\) according to \((c,\delta)\) and put
\[
 J_2(c)=I_{\delta\delta}
 -I_{\delta c}I_{cc}^{-1}I_{c\delta}>0.
\tag{SC7}
\]
For testing \(H_0:\delta=0\) against \(H_1:\delta>0\), the residualized
score test, equivalently the local likelihood-ratio test, is asymptotically
level \(\alpha\) uniformly in \(c\).  At
\((c_N,\delta_N)=(c+u/\sqrt N,v/\sqrt N)\) its limiting power is
\[
 1-\Phi_{\rm N}\{z_{1-\alpha}-v\sqrt{J_2(c)}\}.
\tag{SC8}
\]
This is the Gaussian half-plane power envelope among tests with uniform local
asymptotic level \(\alpha\).

\item There is an estimator \(\widehat{\mathcal R}_N\) of the unordered rate
pair such that, uniformly for bounded \((u,v)\),
\[
 d_{\rm m}\left(
 \widehat{\mathcal R}_N,
 \{\!\{c+u/\sqrt N+\sqrt{v/\sqrt N},
          c+u/\sqrt N-\sqrt{v/\sqrt N}\}\!\}\right)
 =O_P(N^{-1/4}).
\tag{SC9}
\]
The estimated centre is root-\(N\), and the estimator first obtains a
root-\(N\) estimate of \(\delta\) and projects it onto \([0,\infty)\).
Conversely, for every fixed \(c\in(\varepsilon,M)\), \(v_0>0\), and
\(0<a<\sqrt{v_0}/2\),
\[
 \liminf_{N\to\infty}\ \inf_{\widehat{\mathcal R}_N}
 \max_{v\in\{0,v_0\}}
 P_{c,v/\sqrt N}^{(N)}\!\left\{
 N^{1/4}d_{\rm m}(\widehat{\mathcal R}_N,
 \{\!\{c+\sqrt{v/\sqrt N},c-\sqrt{v/\sqrt N}\}\!\})>a
 \right\}>0.
\tag{SC10}
\]
Thus \(N^{-1/4}\) is a matching local minimax rate, with both an upper and a
lower bound.  The sign of \(d\) is not identifiable.
\end{enumerate}
\end{paperthm}

We now impose exactly the isolated-collision restrictions used in the
higher-order result.  Fix \(n\ge2\), \(0<\varepsilon<M<\infty\), and
\(c_0>0\).  The compact double-rate stratum is
\[
 \mathcal C_{n}(\varepsilon,M,c_0)=
 \left\{(\lambda,\lambda,\lambda_3,\ldots,\lambda_n):
 \begin{array}{l}
 \lambda,\lambda_j\in[\varepsilon,M],\\
 |\lambda_j-\lambda|\ge c_0,\\
 |\lambda_i-\lambda_j|\ge c_0\quad(3\le i<j\le n)
 \end{array}\right\}.
\tag{SC11}
\]
Only one double collision is allowed; all other rates are simple and uniformly
separated.  No assertion below covers simultaneous collisions, unknown order,
unknown sampling interval, or nonserial phase-type representations.

\begin{paperthm}[Stationary double-pole experiment for sampled generalized-Erlang renewals]
\label{thm:stationary-serial-double-pole-main}
Fix \(n\), \(\varepsilon,M,c_0\), and known \(\Delta\tau>0\) as above, and
suppose \(\mathcal C_n(\varepsilon,M,c_0)\ne\varnothing\).  For
\(\theta=(\theta_1,\ldots,\theta_n)\), let
\[
 Q(\theta)_{ii}=-\theta_i,\qquad
 Q(\theta)_{i,i-1}=\theta_i\quad(2\le i\le n),
\quad K_\theta=e^{\Delta\tau Q(\theta)},
\quad T_{1,\theta}=(I-K_\theta)\mathbf1e_n^\top.
\tag{SC12}
\]
Let \(P_\theta^{(N)}\) be the stationary length-\(N\) binary record from the
killed-reset kernels \((K_\theta,T_{1,\theta})\).  Near the displayed
stratum use
\[
 c=\frac{\theta_1+\theta_2}{2},\qquad
 \delta=\left(\frac{\theta_1-\theta_2}{2}\right)^2\ge0,
 \qquad \eta=(c,\theta_3,\ldots,\theta_n)\in\RR^{n-1}.
\tag{SC13}
\]
Write \(g_{\eta,\delta}\), \(S_{\eta,\delta}\), and
\(\mu_{\eta,\delta}\) for the Palm gap mass, tail, and mean cycle length,
and at \((\eta_0,0)\) put
\[
 \dot\ell_{\eta,\delta}(k)
 =\nabla_{(\eta,\delta)}\log g_{\eta,\delta}(k)|_{(\eta_0,0)},
 \qquad
 I_n(\eta_0)=\frac1{\mu_{\eta_0,0}}
 \sum_{k\ge0}g_{\eta_0,0}(k)
 \dot\ell_{\eta,\delta}(k)\dot\ell_{\eta,\delta}(k)^\top.
\tag{SC14}
\]
Then:

\begin{enumerate}[label=\textup{(\roman*)},leftmargin=2.6em]
\item For bounded \(u\in\RR^{n-1}\) and \(v\in[0,\infty)\), uniformly over
\((\eta_0,0)\in\mathcal C_n(\varepsilon,M,c_0)\),
\[
 \log\frac{dP_{\eta_0+u/\sqrt N,v/\sqrt N}^{(N)}}
               {dP_{\eta_0,0}^{(N)}}
 =\begin{pmatrix}u\\v\end{pmatrix}^{\!\top}\Delta_{N,\eta_0}
 -\frac12\begin{pmatrix}u\\v\end{pmatrix}^{\!\top}
 I_n(\eta_0)\begin{pmatrix}u\\v\end{pmatrix}
 +o_{P_{\eta_0,0}}(1),
\tag{SC15}
\]
and \(\Delta_{N,\eta_0}\Rightarrow
\mathcal N_n\{0,I_n(\eta_0)\}\) uniformly in bounded Lipschitz distance.
The local stationary-record experiments therefore converge to the Gaussian
shift on \(\RR^{n-1}\times[0,\infty)\).

\item The information is uniformly nondegenerate:
\[
 \inf_{\eta_0\in\mathcal C_n(\varepsilon,M,c_0)}
 \lambda_{\min}\{I_n(\eta_0)\}>0.
\tag{SC16}
\]
This follows from sampled pole order, not merely from population
identifiability: the \(\delta\)-derivative has a nonzero
\(k^3e^{-\lambda\Delta\tau k}\) component; the centre derivative reaches
degree two at that base; and the derivative of each separated rate has a
unique degree-one component at its own exponential base.

\item Partition \(I_n\) according to \((\eta,\delta)\), and set
\[
 J_n=I_{\delta\delta}
 -I_{\delta\eta}I_{\eta\eta}^{-1}I_{\eta\delta}>0.
\tag{SC17}
\]
The residualized score test, or the local likelihood-ratio test, is uniformly
asymptotically level \(\alpha\) for \(H_0:\delta=0\) and attains at
\(\delta_N=v/\sqrt N\) the nuisance-adjusted power envelope
\[
 1-\Phi_{\rm N}\{z_{1-\alpha}-v\sqrt{J_n(\eta_0)}\}.
\tag{SC18}
\]

\item A recurrence estimator based on the first \(2n\) empirical complete-gap
tails gives root-\(N\) estimates of \((\eta,\delta)\), after projection of
\(\delta\) onto \([0,\infty)\).  The induced unordered-multiset estimator
\(\widehat{\mathcal R}_{n,N}\) satisfies, uniformly over the compact stratum
and bounded \((u,v)\),
\[
 d_{\rm m}(\widehat{\mathcal R}_{n,N},
 \{\!\{c_N+\sqrt{\delta_N},c_N-\sqrt{\delta_N},
          \theta_{3,N},\ldots,\theta_{n,N}\}\!\})=O_P(N^{-1/4}),
\tag{SC19}
\]
where \((\eta_N,\delta_N)=(\eta_0+u/\sqrt N,v/\sqrt N)\).  The centre and
all separated rates are root-\(N\).

For every fixed point of the stratum, every \(v_0>0\), and every
\(0<a<\sqrt{v_0}/2\), the analogue of \textup{(SC10)}, with the remaining
rates held fixed, has a strictly positive lower limit.  Hence no estimator is
uniformly \(o_P(N^{-1/4})\) on bounded collision alternatives, while
\textup{(SC19)} attains \(O_P(N^{-1/4})\).  The minimax rate is therefore
matched by both bounds.
\end{enumerate}
\end{paperthm}

.\artifacts\oracle_review_r1.md:33:ResultRatingJustificationTheorem AMEDIUMThe exact combination of gap law, monotone hazard, covariance tail, 1-dependence threshold, and non-Markovianity is useful for this constrained kernel, but follows from elementary two-state spectral and renewal calculations.Theorem BMEDIUMThe three-inclusion quotient formula is the manuscript’s strongest potentially original result, but its distinction from existing order-two DMAP canonical-coordinate theory has not been established.Theorem CLOWThis is a standard regenerative reward CLT with model-specific bookkeeping for the three inclusion statistics.Theorem DLOWIt is the ordinary multivariate delta method applied to Theorems B and C; the more relevant root/rate transport is relegated to Corollary 3.86.Theorem ELOWThe tangent-cone projection limit is standard boundary asymptotics; novelty is limited to a numerical full-rank certificate at one fixed point  $z_0=1/2$ .Theorem 3.33LOWContinuum nonidentifiability follows from standard minimal-realization similarity and openness of the interior Markov cone.Theorem 3.40LOWThis is a separated-batch covariance consistency argument for 1-dependent regenerative rewards.Corollary 3.32, although not formally a theoremMEDIUMRecovery of repeated serial rates from finitely many sampled survival coordinates is potentially useful, but it is closely connected to established confluent-Prony and acyclic DPH canonical-form results.

Overall verdict
There is one plausible tier-raising route, but it requires changing the paperâ€™s mathematical centre.
The target is not another two-state quotient, image equation, fibre parametrization, covariance formula, or model-specific test. Those directions are capped by the existing order-two canonical-representation and weak-equivalence theory. MÃ©szÃ¡rosâ€“Telek already give a canonical representation of the whole order-two DRAP/DMAP class and describe it through four process-level parameters; RamÃ­rez-Coboâ€“Lillo give a complete characterization of weakly equivalent MAP2 processes. Roundcube Webmail+2Roundcube Webmail+2 The manuscript now acknowledges exactly this priority boundary and calls its three-inclusion inverse only a candidate original specialization pending a full reduction.  
The viable upgrade is:

A uniform stationary-record singular limit experiment, with efficient testing and a matching Nâˆ’1/4 minimax rate, for collisions of rates in sampled generalized-Erlang/serial phase-type renewal processes.

This would move the paper from â€œa highly explicit analysis of one constrained D-MAP(2)â€ to a theorem about a standard phase-type family and a genuine nonregular statistical phenomenon. Merely proving the corresponding statement for the current two-state counter would repair an important gap, but would probably not raise the paper by a full tier.
The repairs in the present PDF do matter. The Aâ€“E registry and dependency claims are now coherent; the stopped batch-means argument exposes the correct balance
bNâˆ’1â€‹+bNâ€‹/Nâ€‹,
yielding Nâˆ’1/3; the physical-image proof now supplies uniform tail and covariance-nondegeneracy arguments; and Theorem D gives an exact stationary-record likelihood factorization for its Markov-gap path.      I therefore no longer regard those matters as the principal structural ceiling. The unfilled high-value gap is the one the paper itself identifies: its symmetric-split likelihood is only for a deterministic product of independent Palm gaps, not for the actual stationary finite record. 

1. The single most valuable new result

Why this is the right theorem
It supplies all four properties missing from the current split calculation:


the actual stationary record, rather than a deterministic product of complete gaps;


a standard field object, the sampled generalized-Erlang or serial DPH family;


a complete limit experiment, not one prescribed symmetric path;


a matching lower and upper rate, not merely an expansion exhibiting Nâˆ’1/4.


The manuscript already proves that the first 2n sampled Palm tails identify the serial rate multiset, including repeated rates, but presents that result explicitly as a specialization of generalized-Erlang/Coxian representation theory.  The proposed theorem turns the same collision geometry into a nonregular inference result that is not supplied by population identifiability.
Regular phase-type inference, Fisher information, and MLE calculations already exist, and current dead-time event-detection work proves regular LAN, lower bounds, and efficient MLE/one-step procedures. Welcome to DTU Research Database+2arXiv+2 Therefore the theorem must retain the collision singularity, stationary renewal stopping, quotient parameter, and sharp Nâˆ’1/4 boundary. A merely regular MLE theorem would not raise the tier.

a Gaussian half-plane limit experiment, an efficient test of Î´=0, and a sharp Nâˆ’1/4 rate for the unordered roots.
This should be proved before attempting the n-phase theorem. But as a final headline it remains a singular-boundary calculation for the bespoke two-state counter. General locally conic and loss-of-identifiability theory already provides the conceptual template, and singular hidden-state models can exhibit similarly nonstandard local rates. arXiv It would materially repair the paper, but I would not count on it alone to move the paper up a tier.

2. Reachability from the present machinery
2.1 First-choice theorem: reachable, with one substantial new lemma
I classify it as a long but direct completion plus one genuinely new algebraic-statistical ingredient, not as a wholly different research programme.
Existing parts that feed the proof
A. The serial Palm law and confluent recurrence
Corollary 3.3 already establishes that, with collisions allowed,
Skâ€‹=jâˆ‘â€‹â„“=0âˆ‘mjâ€‹âˆ’1â€‹cjâ„“â€‹kâ„“zjkâ€‹
and that the minimal recurrence polynomial is
jâˆâ€‹(xâˆ’zjâ€‹)mjâ€‹.
It also proves invertibility of the leading Hankel matrix and recovery from S0â€‹,â€¦,S2nâˆ’1â€‹. 
This gives:


the analytic exponential-polynomial representation;


the correct local quotient coordinates near a collision;


a finite-dimensional pilot estimator through sampled tails and recurrence coefficients;


population identifiability of Î´.


B. Regenerative complete-cycle bookkeeping
Theorem C already rewrites stationary-record statistics as a randomly stopped sum of finite-range Palm rewards and controls the endpoint deletion.  Its proof uses:


exponential Palm-gap tails;


KNâ€‹=N/Î¼+OPâ€‹(Nâ€‹);


negligible initial and terminal incomplete cycles;


finite-range or i.i.d. gap CLTs. 


For the likelihood score the reward is simply
â„“Ë™(Gjâ€‹),
which is easier than the adjacent-gap reward used in Theorem C.
C. Exact stationary likelihood treatment
Theorem D now writes the exact stationary-record likelihood, including the initial age and final residual factors, and proves that its endpoint factors are oPâ€‹(1) under Nâˆ’1/2-local perturbations.  Although that formula is written for the Markov-gap alternative, the renewal case is simpler:
LNâ€‹(Î¸)âˆÎ¼Î¸âˆ’1â€‹SÎ¸â€‹(ANâ€‹)j=1âˆKNâ€‹âˆ’1â€‹gÎ¸â€‹(Gjâ€‹)SÎ¸â€‹(RNâ€‹).
The same exponential-tail argument should make the two survival factors and Î¼Î¸âˆ’1â€‹ contribute only oPâ€‹(1) to the local log likelihood.
D. Existing symmetric split expansion
The stated complete-gap result already finds the correct local scaling
p=z+hNâˆ’1/4,s=zâˆ’hNâˆ’1/4,
and identifies h2 rather than its sign. The manuscript correctly limits this result to a product experiment and expressly disclaims stationary-record equivalence.  This supplies the n=2 algebraic prototype.
E. Compact-uniform tail technology
The physical-image theorem proves a uniform bound of the form
Î¸supâ€‹PÎ¸â€‹(Gâ‰¥k)â‰¤C(1+k)eâˆ’Îµk,
and uses it to obtain uniform CLTs, endpoint control, and uniform covariance consistency.  For fixed n, the serial family has the analogous bound with a polynomial factor of degree at most nâˆ’1.
The genuinely new missing ingredient
The key new lemma is a sampled pole-order nondegeneracy theorem.
At a double continuous rate c, write the colliding pair as cÂ±d, with Î´=d2. Its Laplace factor is
(s+c+d)(s+câˆ’d)(c+d)(câˆ’d)â€‹=(s+c)2âˆ’Î´c2âˆ’Î´â€‹.
At Î´=0,
âˆ‚Î´â€‹log(s+c)2âˆ’Î´c2âˆ’Î´â€‹=âˆ’c21â€‹+(s+c)21â€‹.
After multiplication by the base transform:


the Î´-derivative has a pole of order 4 at âˆ’c;


the centre derivative has at most order 3;


derivatives in the separated rates have their distinguishing poles elsewhere.


After sampling, the same distinction becomes an exponential-polynomial distinction:


the collision derivative contains a nonzero k3eâˆ’cÎ”k term;


the centre derivative reaches at most degree 2 at that sampled pole;


other-rate derivatives are supported at different exponential bases.


Consequently, no nontrivial linear combination of the score coordinates can vanish for every k. Since g(k)>0 for every k, this proves positive definiteness of the one-cycle information. Compactness then gives the uniform lower bound.
That argument is strongly suggested by the paperâ€™s confluent-Prony calculation, but it is not currently in the manuscript. It is the theoremâ€™s real model-specific contribution. Everything after itâ€”LAN, efficient-score residualization, contiguity, the power envelope, and the two-point rate lower boundâ€”is standard but must be carried out uniformly and without hiding the stationary endpoints.
What does not materially feed the proof
The following present components are not needed:


the two-state fibre arc;


the (nâˆ’1)2 similarity-orbit calculation;


the Bickelâ€“Kwon Markov tangent;


the complete-visible-law specification guard;


the moving Helmert coupling;


the pointwise metric projection example.


That is an advantage. The proposed theorem has a clean proof spine rather than requiring every existing section.

2.2 Two-state fallback: very reachable, but lower impact
The existing analytic image chart already supplies smooth coordinates
e=0,D=(pâˆ’s)2â‰¥0
through the exchange point and proves that (e,D) has rank two even on the diagonal.  Theorem 1.11 already analyzes Nâˆ’1/4 root splitting at the level of a constraint statistic.  The remaining work is:


write the full stationary renewal likelihood;


differentiate in (m,Î´), rather than in labelled roots;


prove positive information uniformly for z in a compact subset of (0,1);


derive the constrained Gaussian experiment and minimax statements.


That is a direct completion, with high success probability. Its weakness is priority and scope, not feasibility.

2.3 A tempting named-open target that is not reachable
A genuinely major representation-theoretic target would be:
Complete order-three DPH canonical representation theorem
Every minimal DPH distribution of order three admits, according to its real/complex eigenvalue type, a representative in a finite explicit list of Markovian canonical zero patterns; the representative is unique modulo the prescribed state permutations; and explicit necessary-and-sufficient semialgebraic inequalities and a terminating inversion algorithm decide which pattern applies.
Papp and Telek explicitly state that general cyclic DPH canonical representations were unavailable and that full characterization of the order-three class required completing their Theorem 9. Roundcube Webmail+1
This would have very high impact. It is not reachable from the present paper. The current machinery supplies:


a serial positive-real-eigenvalue subclass;


local similarity-orbit geometry at strictly positive minimal kernels;


one explicit order-two cone intersection.


It does not supply:


global boundary stratification;


negative or complex spectral cases;


a finite exhaustive list of order-three Markovian forms;


global semialgebraic existence and uniqueness;


control of nonminimal degenerations.


The manuscript itself identifies these as open interfaces rather than consequences.  Reaching the DPH3 target would require new invariant-cone and semialgebraic geometry, not a longer application of the existing similarity argument.

3. Probability Ã— tier-impact ranking
I use a 0â€“10 tier-impact scale. The product is
(successÂ probabilityÂ asÂ aÂ fraction)Ã—(impactÂ score).
RankTargetReferee-grade success probabilityTier impactProductJudgment1Uniform stationary double-pole LAN, optimal testing, and sharp Nâˆ’1/4 recovery for sampled generalized-Erlang/serial DPH(n) records60%9.0/105.40The only target with a realistic chance of raising the paper a full tier2Same complete stationary singular experiment only for the present two-state model85%4.5/103.83Important repair and prerequisite, but unlikely to raise the tier alone3Complete order-three DPH canonical representation8%10/100.80Major result, but current machinery is structurally inadequate
The 60% estimate for the first target assumes the theorem is restricted to:


fixed n;


one isolated double collision;


all other rates uniformly separated;


compact positive rate sets;


known Î”.


Those restrictions retain mathematical significance. Allowing arbitrary simultaneous collisions, unknown order, or nonserial phase-type representations would reduce the success probability sharply and would require additional ideas.
A three-inclusion minimality theorem, a broader rank-one-reset quotient, or a global restatement in canonical DMAP2 coordinates would have success probabilities above 80%, but impact scores at most 3/10. I do not regard them as competitive tier-raising targets.

4. Which tier-raising levers apply?
(a) Settling a named open problem in the literature: does not apply
I found no named open problem that is both:


genuinely connected to the present mechanisms, and


realistically solvable using them.


Two explicit nearby benchmarks illustrate the mismatch.
First, the order-three DPH canonical problem is relevant to phase-type representation, but the paper has only local interior orbit geometry and a serial subclass; it lacks the global boundary and spectral analysis needed for that problem. Roundcube Webmail+1
Second, Asanjarani and Nazarathy formulate the conjectures that every irreducible MMPP has squared coefficient of variation at least one and satisfies a specified stochastic order. å‰‘æ¡¥å¤§å­¦å‡ºç‰ˆç¤¾ These concern nonrenewal Markov-modulated Poisson processes of arbitrary order. The deterministic-reset renewal reduction in this manuscript does not address them.
Resolution condition: lever (a) would become available only after importing a fundamentally different global representation or MMPP argument. It is not a responsible target for the present project.

(b) Proving something about the fieldâ€™s standard objects: applies, and is essential
The target should be stated for:

sampled generalized-Erlang distributions, equivalently serial DPH(n) killed-reset renewal records with one repeated rate.

Generalized-Erlang/Coxian and phase-type distributions are standard objects. The present paper already invokes their representation theory, but only to obtain a finite-coordinate population-identifiability corollary. 
The new result must be advertised as a theorem about singular statistical inference for sampled generalized-Erlang renewal processes. The two-state killed-reset counter should become the n=2 worked example.
This lever is the main source of the proposed theoremâ€™s tier impact. Without it, even a perfect stationary n=2 LAN theorem remains too model-local.

(c) Removing a hypothesis: applies in two precise ways, but not to the reset condition
Hypothesis removal 1: deterministic product sample â†’ stationary record
The current singular calculation assumes
mNâ€‹(z)=âŒŠN/Î¼zâ€‹âŒ‹
independent complete gaps. The paper expressly says this is not a stationary-record endpoint equivalence.  Removing that sampling idealization is mathematically meaningful because the observed object throughout the paper is the stationary binary record.
Hypothesis removal 2: two phases â†’ arbitrary fixed serial order
The present symmetric-split calculation is two-rate. Extending the theorem to one isolated collision in an arbitrary fixed generalized-Erlang order makes the phenomenon representation-theoretic rather than device-specific.
Hypothesis that should not be advertised as removable: deterministic post-click reset
The manuscript correctly proves that allowing a positive marked transition into R destroys the fixed Palm restart and invalidates the renewal product, covariance transfer, and quotient route in general.  Removing that hypothesis would require hidden-Markov or Markov-renewal likelihood theory and a different identifiability analysis. It is not a strengthening obtainable from the current proof.

(d) Sharpness or matching bounds: applies decisively
This is the second essential lever.
The target must prove both:
noÂ uniformÂ oPâ€‹(Nâˆ’1/4)Â recoveryÂ ofÂ theÂ collidingÂ rates,
and
existenceÂ ofÂ anÂ OPâ€‹(Nâˆ’1/4)Â recoveryÂ procedure.
The lower bound follows from contiguous alternatives
Î´Nâ€‹=v/Nâ€‹,âˆ£Î¸1,Nâ€‹âˆ’Î¸2,Nâ€‹âˆ£=2vâ€‹Nâˆ’1/4,
combined with the Gaussian limit experiment. The upper bound should come from a root-N estimator of the smooth quotient coordinate Î´, obtained either by:


a local constrained MLE;


a Hankel/Prony pilot followed by a one-step efficient correction; or


a smooth minimum-distance estimator of recurrence coefficients.


The theorem should also state and attain the one-sided local testing envelope. That separates it from a mere Taylor expansion showing that h2 appears.
The moving-dimensional Helmert â€œif and only ifâ€ boundary, even if fully correct, is not the sharpness result I would choose as the paperâ€™s upgrade. It concerns the validity of one high-dimensional Gaussian approximation inside a supplementary construction. The collision lower/upper bound concerns the fundamental recoverability of a standard phase-type parameter and therefore has substantially wider priority value.

5. Strongest remaining higher-tier objection
Even after the proposed theorem is proved, the hardest credible referee objection would be:

â€œAfter reparametrizing by squared root separation, the new result is an application of ordinary one-sided LAN to a smooth renewal family. The rest of the manuscript is still an overgrown union of explicit DMAP2 algebra, weak-equivalence geometry, specification tests, semiparametric Markov alternatives, growing-dimensional Gaussian coupling, and a projection example. The paper has not demonstrated that its flagship theorem is either general enough or central enough to justify this architecture.â€

That objection would be serious. General phase-type Fisher-information calculations already exist, regular dead-time event-detection LAN and efficient estimators now exist, and generic boundary and loss-of-identifiability theory is standard. Welcome to DTU Research Database+2arXiv+2 The paper therefore cannot claim priority merely for placing familiar boundary asymptotics into new notation.
What would resolve it
1. Make the n-phase theorem, not the two-state corollary, the headline
The paper must prove the pole-order nondegeneracy uniformly for an isolated double pole in arbitrary fixed serial order. That is the part not supplied automatically by generic boundary theory.
2. Include the complete stationary experiment
The theorem must cover the actual stationary binary record, including random renewal stopping and endpoint censoring. A proof only for independent complete gaps will not suffice.
3. Include both optimal testing and matching estimation rates
A LAN display alone is not enough. The paper needs:


efficient nuisance residualization;


the exact one-sided local power envelope;


a contiguity-based Nâˆ’1/4 lower bound;


an explicit rate-attaining estimator.


4. Rebuild the article around one proof chain
A credible higher-tier organization would be:


sampled generalized-Erlang renewal model and quotient collision coordinates;


analytic continuation and sampled pole-order nondegeneracy;


stationary likelihood and uniform LAN;


Gaussian cone experiment and efficient testing;


sharp estimation rate;


two-state killed-reset counter as an explicit corollary.


5. Remove or separate material that does not serve that chain
From the main paper I would move out, or delete:


the complete-visible-law distributional-distance test;


the physical-image specification test;


the fixed-marginal Markov tangent;


the moving Helmert layer;


the infinite weighted score test;


the pointwise projection example;


the general (nâˆ’1)2 similarity-orbit discussion.


The exact two-state fibre arc could remain as a short population contrast, but it should not compete with the singular-inference theorem for headline status. The present comparison table already concedes that the arc is a cone-intersection corollary of weak-equivalence theory and that the higher-state dimension statement is only a local Lie-orbit result.  
6. Finish the canonical-coordinate priority reduction
The three-inclusion quotient must either be explicitly derived from the MÃ©szÃ¡rosâ€“Telek coordinates and labelled as a useful specialization, or be shown to contain a genuinely new minimal-observation statement not present there. Until then it should not remain a coequal flagship.
7. Remove any physical-system implication not supported by validation
The current PDF correctly says that the sampled counter is an interpretation of the constrained kernel, not an independently analysed physical system.  A mathematically recentered article should maintain that restraint. A higher-tier physical or engineering claim would require a real detector model, simulation study, or experimental comparison beyond the present theorem package.

Final go/no-go assessment
Go on the n-phase stationary double-pole theorem, beginning with the n=2 stationary proof as an internal milestone.
No-go on trying to raise the tier through further two-state quotient formulas, additional image tests, longer fibre classifications inside the same constrained cone, more delta methods, or a polished proof of the existing product-gap split expansion. Those may improve correctness but do not alter the paperâ€™s priority class.
The structural ceiling of the current architecture is that deterministic reset converts the problem to a renewal distribution, while the population representation questions then reduce largely to standard order-two canonical coordinates, minimal-realization equivalence, or generalized-Erlang representation. The proposed stationary singular theorem escapes that ceiling because it asks a different and genuinely sharper question: what is the complete local statistical experiment, and the exact recoverability boundary, when a standard phase-type representation loses root identifiability through pole collision?

 succeeded in 182ms:
\subsection{Stationary likelihood reduction and the two-state proof}
\label{subsec:stationary-renewal-lan-proof-main}

We use the following renewal lemma with a finite-dimensional parameter
\(q\).  Its uniform formulation is included because replacing a deterministic
number of Palm gaps by the stationary record is essential here.

\begin{lemma}[Compact-uniform stationary renewal LAN]
\label{lem:compact-uniform-stationary-renewal-lan-main}
Let \(\mathcal Q_0\) be compact.  Suppose that, on a fixed one-sided
neighbourhood of \(\mathcal Q_0\), the integer-valued Palm gap masses
\(g_q(k)>0\) satisfy the following bounds for some \(r<1\):
\[
 \sup_q g_q(k)\le C(1+k)^m r^k,
 \qquad
 \sup_q|\partial_q^a\log g_q(k)|\le C_a(1+k)^{m_a},
 \quad 1\le |a|\le3.
\tag{SL1}
\]
Assume the same type of polynomial bound for the first two derivatives of
\(\log S_q(k)\), where \(S_q(k)=\sum_{j\ge k}g_q(j)\), and put
\(\mu_q=\sum_{k\ge0}S_q(k)\).  If
\[
 I(q)=\mu_q^{-1}\sum_{k\ge0}g_q(k)
 \dot\ell_q(k)\dot\ell_q(k)^\top,
 \qquad \dot\ell_q=\nabla_q\log g_q,
\tag{SL2}
\]
is positive definite on \(\mathcal Q_0\), then the stationary binary renewal
record is LAN, uniformly over \(q\in\mathcal Q_0\) and bounded admissible
local vectors \(h\):
\[
 \log\frac{dP_{q+h/\sqrt N}^{(N)}}{dP_q^{(N)}}
 =h^\top\Delta_{N,q}-\frac12h^\top I(q)h+o_{P_q}(1),
 \qquad
 \Delta_{N,q}\Rightarrow\mathcal N\{0,I(q)\}.
\tag{SL3}
\]
The convergence of the central sequence and the remainder are compact-uniform.
The conclusion remains valid when some coordinates of \(h\) are restricted
to a closed convex cone.
\end{lemma}

\begin{proof}
Let the clicks in \([0,N-1]\) be
\(0\le C_1<\cdots<C_J<N\), set
\[
 A=C_1,\qquad R=N-1-C_J,
 \qquad G_j=C_{j+1}-C_j-1\quad(1\le j<J).
\]
For \(J\ge1\), stationary Palm inversion gives the exact record probability
\[
 P_q^{(N)}(A_0,\ldots,A_{N-1})
 =\frac1{\mu_q}S_q(A)
   \prod_{j=1}^{J-1}g_q(G_j)S_q(R).
\tag{SL4}
\]
Indeed, \(S_q(A)/\mu_q\) is the equilibrium forward-recurrence mass of the
first click, the displayed gaps are complete Palm cycles, and after the last
click the absence of another click contributes \(S_q(R)\).  The no-click
event is bounded by \(CN^{m+1}r^N\), uniformly in \(q\), by summing over the
equilibrium cycle containing the whole window.

The common exponential envelope gives, uniformly in \(q\),
\[
 J/N\longrightarrow1/\mu_q,\qquad
 J=N/\mu_q+O_{P_q}(\sqrt N),\qquad
 \max\{A,R,G_1,\ldots,G_{J-1}\}=O_{P_q}(\log N).
\tag{SL5}
\]
The first assertion is the uniform renewal law of large numbers; the second
follows from the uniform finite-variance renewal CLT or maximal inequality;
and the last follows by a union bound from \textup{(SL1)}.  The polynomial
log-tail derivative bound and \textup{(SL5)} imply, for bounded \(h\),
\[
 \log\frac{\mu_q}{\mu_{q+h/\sqrt N}}
 +\log\frac{S_{q+h/\sqrt N}(A)}{S_q(A)}
 +\log\frac{S_{q+h/\sqrt N}(R)}{S_q(R)}=o_{P_q}(1)
\tag{SL6}
\]
uniformly.  Thus the stationary endpoints do not alter the local experiment.

Taylor expansion of the complete-gap part of \textup{(SL4)} yields
\[
 \frac1{\sqrt N}\sum_{j=1}^{J-1}h^\top\dot\ell_q(G_j)
 +\frac1{2N}\sum_{j=1}^{J-1}
 h^\top\ddot\ell_q(G_j)h+o_{P_q}(1).
\tag{SL7}
\]
The third-order remainder is uniform because \textup{(SL1)} supplies a
common integrable polynomial envelope, so its expectation is \(O(N^{-1/2})\).
Differentiating \(\sum_kg_q(k)=1\), justified by \textup{(SL1)}, gives
\[
 \EE_q\dot\ell_q(G)=0,\qquad
 \EE_q\ddot\ell_q(G)=-\EE_q\{
 \dot\ell_q(G)\dot\ell_q(G)^\top\}.
\tag{SL8}
\]
The uniform law of large numbers, \textup{(SL5)}, and \textup{(SL8)} turn the
second term in \textup{(SL7)} into \(-h^\top I(q)h/2+o_P(1)\).
The stopped i.i.d. score CLT gives
\[
 \Delta_{N,q}:=N^{-1/2}\sum_{j=1}^{J-1}\dot\ell_q(G_j)
 \Rightarrow\mathcal N\{0,I(q)\}.
\tag{SL9}
\]
All assertions are uniform by the subsequence criterion: every sequence in
the compact base set has a convergent subsequence, and the common exponential
and polynomial envelopes give Lindeberg, uniform integrability, endpoint
control, and the Taylor remainder along it.  Combining
\textup{(SL4)}--\textup{(SL9)} proves \textup{(SL3)}.  Restricting local
coordinates to a cone changes only the parameter set of the limit experiment.
\end{proof}

\begin{proof}[Proof of \Cref{thm:stationary-two-state-collision-main}]
Let \(W=X_1+X_2\), where the two independent exponential holding times have
rates \(c+d\) and \(c-d\).  Its Laplace transform and the transform of its
continuous survival function are
\[
 \phi_{c,\delta}(s)
 =\frac{c^2-\delta}{(s+c)^2-\delta},
 \qquad
 H_{c,\delta}(s)=\frac{1-\phi_{c,\delta}(s)}s.
\tag{T21}
\]
Differentiation at \(\delta=0\) gives exactly \textup{(SC5)} and
\[
 \partial_cH_{c,0}(s)=-\frac{2c}{(s+c)^3}.
\tag{T22}
\]
Inverse Laplace transformation and sampling at \(t=k\Delta\tau\) show that
\(\partial_\delta S_{c,0}(k)\) has a nonzero cubic coefficient at
\(e^{-c\Delta\tau k}\), whereas \(\partial_cS_{c,0}(k)\) has degree two.
Taking first differences preserves linear independence.  Since every
\(g_{c,0}(k)>0\), a linear combination of the two scores can vanish in
\(L^2(g_{c,0})\) only if the corresponding linear combination of the mass
derivatives, hence of the tail derivatives, vanishes at every \(k\).  The
distinct polynomial degrees then force both coefficients to be zero.  This
proves pointwise positive definiteness in \textup{(SC3)}.

On \([\varepsilon,M]\), partial-fraction inversion gives common bounds
\[
 |\partial_c^a\partial_\delta^b S_{c,\delta}(k)|
 \le C_{a,b}(1+k)^{m_{a,b}}e^{-\varepsilon\Delta\tau k/2}
\tag{T23}
\]
on a fixed neighbourhood with \(c\pm\sqrt\delta>0\).  The corresponding
log-mass and log-tail derivatives have polynomial bounds; alternatively these
bounds follow by differentiating the convolution law and conditioning on
\(W>k\Delta\tau\) or on the sampled absorption bin.  Continuity of
\(I_2(c)\), dominated by \textup{(T23)}, and compactness now give
\textup{(SC6)}.  Applying
\Cref{lem:compact-uniform-stationary-renewal-lan-main} with
\(q=(c,\delta)\) proves \textup{(SC4)} and the half-plane limit.

For completeness, the efficient central sequence for the collision
coordinate is
\[
 \Delta_{\delta\cdot c,N}
 =\Delta_{\delta,N}-I_{\delta c}I_{cc}^{-1}\Delta_{c,N},
 \qquad \Var(\Delta_{\delta\cdot c,N})\longrightarrow J_2(c).
\tag{T24}
\]
Replacing \(c\) and the information entries by compact-uniform consistent
null estimators changes the standardized statistic by \(o_P(1)\).  The third
lemma gives mean \(v\sqrt{J_2(c)}\) under the local alternative.  The
Neyman--Pearson lemma in the residual one-dimensional Gaussian shift gives
\textup{(SC8)} and its envelope; the one-sided likelihood-ratio statistic is
the square of the positive part of the same standardized score.  This is the
standard Gaussian-cone conclusion
\cite{Chernoff1954LikelihoodRatio,SelfLiang1987NonstandardLRT,
vanDerVaart1998AsymptoticStatistics}.

We next give an explicit rate-attaining estimator.  From the complete gaps in
the stationary record define
\[
 \widehat S_k=K_N^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{K_N}\mathbf1_{\{G_j\ge k\}},
 \qquad 0\le k\le3,
\tag{T25}
\]
with an arbitrary fixed value on \(K_N=0\).  The endpoint deletion and the
stopped i.i.d. CLT give
\(\max_{k\le3}|\widehat S_k-S_k|=O_P(N^{-1/2})\) uniformly.  The order-two
tail recurrence is
\[
 S_{k+2}-A S_{k+1}+B S_k=0,\qquad
 A=z_1+z_2,\quad B=z_1z_2,\quad z_i=e^{-\theta_i\Delta\tau}.
\tag{T26}
\]
Its leading Hankel matrix is nonsingular at the double pole by
\Cref{cor:nstate-serial-all-collision-identifiability-main}; compactness makes
the inverse uniform.  Solving the empirical version of \textup{(T26)} gives
\((\widehat A,\widehat B)-(A,B)=O_P(N^{-1/2})\).

In a neighbourhood of \(A=2z,B=z^2\), the functions
\[
 c=-\frac1{2\Delta\tau}\log B,\qquad
 \delta=\frac1{(\Delta\tau)^2}
 \left[\operatorname{arcosh}\left(\frac{A}{2\sqrt B}\right)\right]^2
\tag{T27}
\]
are real analytic after continuing the square of \(\operatorname{arcosh}\)
through one.  Let \(\widetilde\delta_N\) be this analytic continuation and
put \(\widehat\delta_N=(\widetilde\delta_N)^+\).  Then
\[
 \widehat c_N-c_N=O_P(N^{-1/2}),\qquad
 \widehat\delta_N-\delta_N=O_P(N^{-1/2})
\tag{T28}
\]
uniformly on bounded collision alternatives.  Because
\(|\sqrt{x^+}-\sqrt{y}|\le\sqrt{|x-y|}\) for \(y\ge0\), the pair
\(\{\!\{\widehat c_N\pm\sqrt{\widehat\delta_N}\}\!\}\) satisfies
\textup{(SC9)}.

Finally compare \(v=0\) and \(v=v_0\).  Their rate multisets are separated
by \(\sqrt{v_0}N^{-1/4}\), while \textup{(SC4)} makes the two sequences of
record laws mutually contiguous and gives a nondegenerate finite Gaussian
likelihood ratio.  If an estimator made the two probabilities in
\textup{(SC10)} tend to zero for an \(a<\sqrt{v_0}/2\), assigning the record
to the closer of the two candidate multisets would consistently distinguish
these contiguous experiments, a contradiction.  This proves
\textup{(SC10)} and completes the two-state result.
\end{proof}


 succeeded in 182ms:
\subsection{Pole-order nondegeneracy and the fixed-order serial proof}
\label{subsec:serial-pole-order-proof-main}

\begin{lemma}[Sampled isolated-double-pole nondegeneracy]
\label{lem:sampled-double-pole-nondegeneracy-main}
On \(\mathcal C_n(\varepsilon,M,c_0)\), the Palm gap family is analytic in
\((\eta,\delta)\) on a fixed one-sided neighbourhood of \(\delta=0\), obeys
the bounds in \textup{(SL1)}, and its one-cycle score coordinates are linearly
independent.  Consequently \textup{(SC16)} holds.
\end{lemma}

\begin{proof}
Let \(W=X_1+\cdots+X_n\), where the independent exponential holding times
have rates \(c+\sqrt\delta,c-\sqrt\delta,\theta_3,\ldots,\theta_n\), and put
\[
 B_\eta(s)=\prod_{j=3}^n\frac{\theta_j}{s+\theta_j}.
\]
The empty product is one.  The Laplace transform of the continuous survival
function \(\overline F_{\eta,\delta}(t)=P(W>t)\) is
\[
 H_{\eta,\delta}(s)=\frac1s\left\{1-
 \frac{c^2-\delta}{(s+c)^2-\delta}B_\eta(s)\right\}.
\tag{P1}
\]
At a base point with \(c=\lambda\), direct differentiation gives
\[
 \partial_\delta H_{\eta,0}(s)
 =B_\eta(s)\frac{s+2\lambda}{(s+\lambda)^4},
 \qquad
 \partial_cH_{\eta,0}(s)
 =-\frac{2\lambda B_\eta(s)}{(s+\lambda)^3},
\tag{P2}
\]
and, for \(j\ge3\),
\[
 \partial_{\theta_j}H_{\eta,0}(s)
 =-\frac{\lambda^2}{(s+\lambda)^2}
 \frac{1}{(s+\theta_j)^2}
 \prod_{\substack{\ell=3\\\ell\ne j}}^n
 \frac{\theta_\ell}{s+\theta_\ell}.
\tag{P3}
\]
The displayed expression follows after differentiating
\(\theta_j/(s+\theta_j)\) and cancelling its factor \(s\) against the outer
\(1/s\); all remaining numerator factors are nonzero at the relevant
separated poles.

Uniform separation makes \(B_\eta(-\lambda)\ne0\).  Thus
\(\partial_\delta H\) has an exact order-four pole at \(-\lambda\), with
nonzero leading coefficient \(\lambda B_\eta(-\lambda)\), while
\(\partial_cH\) has order three there.  At \(-\theta_j\), the derivative in
\(\theta_j\) has order two, and every other coordinate derivative has at most
order one there.  Inverse Laplace transformation therefore gives the following
distinguishing sampled terms:
\[
 \begin{array}{c|c}
 \text{coordinate}&\text{term not attainable by the remaining coordinates}\ \\
 \hline
 \delta& k^3e^{-\lambda\Delta\tau k}\\
 c& k^2e^{-\lambda\Delta\tau k}\quad\text{after the \(\delta\) coefficient is zero}\\
 \theta_j& k e^{-\theta_j\Delta\tau k}\quad(3\le j\le n).
 \end{array}
\tag{P4}
\]
All leading coefficients in \textup{(P4)} are nonzero.

Suppose \(a^\top\dot\ell_{\eta,\delta}=0\) in
\(L^2(g_{\eta,0})\).  Full support gives
\(a^\top\partial g_{\eta,0}(k)=0\) for every \(k\).  Absolute summability
then gives the same identity for the tail derivatives.  Exponential-polynomial
sequences at distinct bases are linearly independent: applying successively
the difference operators \(E-z_j\), or equivalently using their rational
generating functions, separates each base and its highest polynomial degree.
The cubic term at \(e^{-\lambda\Delta\tau}\) first forces
\(a_\delta=0\); the unique linear term at every separated base forces
\(a_{\theta_j}=0\); and the remaining quadratic term at the collision base
forces \(a_c=0\).  The scores are therefore linearly independent.

It remains to record uniform analytic control.  Formula \textup{(P1)} is
rational and analytic in \((\eta,\delta)\) on a common complex neighbourhood
whose pole clusters remain separated by compactness and \(c_0\).  Partial
fractions, including the confluent pole at \(-c\), give for every fixed
derivative order
\[
 \sup_{(\eta,\delta)}
 |\partial_{\eta,\delta}^a S_{\eta,\delta}(k)|
 \le C_a(1+k)^{m_a}e^{-\varepsilon\Delta\tau k/2}.
\tag{P5}
\]
First differences give the same bound for \(g\).  Ratios by \(S\) or \(g\)
have polynomial growth uniformly: this follows directly from the finite
confluent expansion on the separated pole clusters, and also from the
conditional holding-time score representation of these derivatives.  Hence
the log derivatives through order three satisfy \textup{(SL1)}.  Dominated
summation makes the information continuous.  Pointwise positive definiteness,
continuity, and compactness yield the strictly positive uniform minimum in
\textup{(SC16)}.
\end{proof}

\begin{lemma}[Uniform quotient estimator from sampled tails]
\label{lem:serial-collision-recurrence-estimator-main}
Under the restrictions of \Cref{thm:stationary-serial-double-pole-main}, there
are estimators \((\widehat\eta_N,\widehat\delta_N)\), with
\(\widehat\delta_N\ge0\), such that for every bounded set of local parameters
\[
 \widehat\eta_N-\eta_N=O_P(N^{-1/2}),
 \qquad
 \widehat\delta_N-\delta_N=O_P(N^{-1/2})
\tag{P6}
\]
uniformly over the compact stratum.  The induced rate multiset satisfies
\textup{(SC19)}.
\end{lemma}

\begin{proof}
Delete the initial and terminal incomplete cycles and write the observed
complete gaps as \(G_1,\ldots,G_{K_N}\).  Define
\[
 \widehat S_k=K_N^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{K_N}\mathbf1_{\{G_j\ge k\}},
 \qquad 0\le k\le2n-1,
\tag{P7}
\]
with arbitrary fixed values on \(K_N=0\).  The common exponential moment,
the stopped i.i.d. CLT, and \(K_N/N\to1/\mu\) imply
\[
 \max_{0\le k\le2n-1}|\widehat S_k-S_k|=O_P(N^{-1/2})
\tag{P8}
\]
uniformly over the stratum and bounded local alternatives.

Write the monic recurrence polynomial as
\[
 \chi(x)=x^n+a_{n-1}x^{n-1}+\cdots+a_0
 =\prod_{i=1}^n(x-z_i),\qquad z_i=e^{-\theta_i\Delta\tau}.
\tag{P9}
\]
The equations
\[
 S_{k+n}+\sum_{j=0}^{n-1}a_jS_{k+j}=0,\qquad0\le k<n,
\tag{P10}
\]
have coefficient matrix \((S_{i+j})_{0\le i,j<n}\).  It is nonsingular even
at collisions by
\Cref{cor:nstate-serial-all-collision-identifiability-main}.  Its determinant
has a positive minimum in absolute value on the compact stratum; the same is
true on a sufficiently small common neighbourhood.  Solving the empirical
version of \textup{(P10)} and using \textup{(P8)} therefore gives
\[
 \max_j|\widehat a_j-a_j|=O_P(N^{-1/2})
\tag{P11}
\]
uniformly.

Uniform separation permits disjoint fixed contours around the double root and
the \(n-2\) simple roots of \(\chi\).  The argument principle, or the
implicit-function theorem applied to the contour power sums, makes every
simple root and the two elementary symmetric functions \(A=z_1+z_2\),
\(B=z_1z_2\) of the double cluster analytic functions of the polynomial
coefficients on these neighbourhoods.  Thus their empirical versions are
root-\(N\) by \textup{(P11)}.  The separated rates are
\(-\log z_j/\Delta\tau\), while the double-cluster quotient coordinates are
the analytic functions in \textup{(T27)}.  Define the estimator by these
local analytic maps whenever \(\widehat\chi\) is in their common
neighbourhood, use any fixed value otherwise, and project the continued
\(\widetilde\delta_N\) onto \([0,\infty)\).  The exceptional event has
probability tending to zero uniformly, and projection is nonexpansive, which
proves \textup{(P6)}.

Finally form the estimated double pair
\(\widehat c_N\pm\sqrt{\widehat\delta_N}\) and the estimated separated
rates.  The square-root inequality used after \textup{(T28)} gives
\(O_P(N^{-1/4})\) for the colliding pair, while all other components are
root-\(N\).  The separation \(c_0\) fixes the optimal matching with
probability tending to one uniformly, proving \textup{(SC19)}.
\end{proof}

\begin{proof}[Proof of \Cref{thm:stationary-serial-double-pole-main}]
The serial killed-reset construction makes the visible record a stationary
renewal record whose Palm cycle is the sampled absorption bin of
\(W=X_1+\cdots+X_n\).  In particular,
\[
 S_{\eta,\delta}(k)=P_{\eta,\delta}
 \{W>k\Delta\tau\},\qquad
 g_{\eta,\delta}(k)=S_{\eta,\delta}(k)-S_{\eta,\delta}(k+1).
\tag{G1}
\]
\Cref{lem:sampled-double-pole-nondegeneracy-main} verifies every analytic,
tail, and information hypothesis of
\Cref{lem:compact-uniform-stationary-renewal-lan-main}.  Applying that lemma
with \(q=(\eta,\delta)\) proves \textup{(SC15)}--\textup{(SC16)}.  Notice
that the proof uses the exact stationary factorization \textup{(SL4)}: its
random number of complete gaps supplies the factor \(1/\mu\) in
\textup{(SC14)}, and its two survival factors and \(1/\mu\) are
\(o_P(1)\) in the local log likelihood.  Thus no deterministic-product
equivalence is being assumed.

For testing, define the efficient collision central sequence
\[
 \Delta_{\delta\cdot\eta,N}
 =\Delta_{\delta,N}
 -I_{\delta\eta}I_{\eta\eta}^{-1}\Delta_{\eta,N}.
\tag{G2}
\]
It has null variance \(J_n\), which is strictly positive by the Schur
complement of \textup{(SC16)}.  Under
\((\eta_N,\delta_N)=(\eta_0+u/\sqrt N,v/\sqrt N)\), the third lemma gives
mean \(vJ_n\); the nuisance shift cancels in \textup{(G2)}.  A null fit from
\Cref{lem:serial-collision-recurrence-estimator-main}, followed by evaluation
of the scores and information at \((\widehat\eta_N,0)\), changes
\(\Delta_{\delta\cdot\eta,N}/\sqrt{J_n}\) by \(o_P(1)\) uniformly.  Rejecting
above \(z_{1-\alpha}\) therefore gives \textup{(SC18)}.  The Gaussian
Neyman--Pearson bound after residualizing the nuisance proves the asserted
power envelope; maximizing the local quadratic likelihood over
\(v\ge0\) yields the same rejection rule.

The estimator lemma proves the upper rate \textup{(SC19)}, including the
root-\(N\) rate for the centre, collision square, and all noncolliding rates.
For the lower bound, fix a base point and hold \(\eta=\eta_0\) fixed while
comparing \(v=0\) with \(v=v_0>0\).  Their unordered rate multisets are
separated by exactly \(\sqrt{v_0}N^{-1/4}\) in \(d_{\rm m}\).  The LAN
likelihood ratio in \textup{(SC15)} converges to a finite nondegenerate
Gaussian likelihood ratio, so these two sequences are mutually contiguous
and no test separates them consistently.  An estimator with
\(o_P(N^{-1/4})\) error under both sequences would, by choosing the closer
candidate multiset, provide such a test.  More quantitatively, for every
\(a<\sqrt{v_0}/2\) the maximum of the two error probabilities has a strictly
positive lower limit, exactly as in \textup{(SC10)}.  This proves the local
minimax lower bound.  Together with \textup{(SC19)} it establishes the
matching \(N^{-1/4}\) rate and completes the theorem.
\end{proof}

 succeeded in 185ms:
Write a question putting the NEW stationary double-pole theorem to the external expert as a
referee, and write it ONLY to tools/chatgpt-oracle/sprint/ask/A8_singular.txt (relative to
D:\omega\automath). No preamble, no commentary, no other files. Self-contained.

BACKGROUND, honestly. He rejected this paper for Journal of Physics Communications, on
priority (Bickel-Kwon omitted), on proof gaps, and because the sampled-counter physics served
only as model interpretation. Those were repaired and it was retargeted to Methodology and
Computing in Applied Probability. Asked what would raise the tier, he said the mathematical
centre had to move off the constrained two-state model, since further two-state quotients are
capped by Meszaros-Telek's canonical representation of the whole order-two DRAP/DMAP class and
by Ramirez-Cobo, Lillo and Wiper's characterization of weakly equivalent MAP2. He ranked the
move at 60% x 9.0 as the only realistic full-tier target. It is now done.

State precisely, reading from article_singular_results.tex and its proof files:
 - the new section on stationary collision experiments for sampled SERIAL PHASE-TYPE renewals,
   and the headline stationary double-pole experiment for sampled generalized-Erlang renewals;
 - the LAN / singular limit experiment statement;
 - that N^{-1/4} is a MATCHING local minimax rate with BOTH an upper and a lower bound, giving
   the two-point lower-bound statement as it appears, and that the sign of d is not
   identifiable;
 - that the N^{-1/4} rate arises from root-N estimation of the squared separation composed
   with the singular square-root map, and that the earlier N^{-1/3} from the stopped
   batch-means argument is NOT reused as the collision rate but remains the rate of that
   separate bandwidth problem;
 - the replaced machinery: sampled pole-order nondegeneracy in place of covariance
   nondegeneracy, using that the collision score has a unique k^3 e^{-lambda dtau k} term with
   the centre reaching degree two at that base while each separated-rate score has a unique
   k e^{-theta_j dtau k} term; the exact stationary likelihood with random stopping and
   endpoint factors in place of the deterministic Palm-product split; and the new recurrence
   estimator and contiguity lower bound.

State the scope restrictions exactly as the manuscript states them: one double collision only,
all other rates simple and uniformly separated, and no claim covering simultaneous collisions,
unknown order, unknown sampling interval or nonserial phase-type representations. Say that
these are the conditions he himself attached to his 60% estimate and that we adopted them
rather than reaching for generality he had not costed.

Say the results are analytic, that numerical batteries are sanity checks only and were not
used as evidence, and note his own earlier warning that for infinite-sample theorems numerics
can never substitute for a uniform remainder argument.

Then ask him, as a referee:
 1. Is the proof correct? Name any step he cannot follow, in particular the pole-order
    nondegeneracy argument, the exact stationary likelihood with random stopping, and the
    contiguity lower bound.
 2. Is the minimax claim honest - is the lower bound a genuine matching bound at N^{-1/4}, or
    does it establish less than "matching"?
 3. Does this actually move the centre off the two-state model in the sense he meant, or is it
    still a constrained special case wearing phase-type clothing?
 4. Is it new? Given that six of our seven papers were found to have priority omissions, treat
    this as critical: does a stationary double-pole LAN with an N^{-1/4} rate for sampled
    phase-type renewals already exist in the singular-experiment or phase-type literature?
 5. With this in place, what is the honest tier and venue judgement now?

Tell him a hard rejection with reasons is more useful than a soft acceptance, and that every
objection should come with what would resolve it.

Context that must go into the question: a previous round asked this expert where the real opportunity was, and the honest answer was that no named open problem in the neighbourhood is reachable with the present machinery. So the question is no longer how to raise the tier. It is whether what we have is publishable as it stands, and what a referee at the target journal would demand.

The question must:
 - state the paper's main theorems precisely and completely, with hypotheses, in standard language;
 - state plainly what is NOT proved, including any claim that was refused or withdrawn during development;
 - separate clearly what is imported and cited from what is ours;
 - give the machine-verification evidence.

Then ask him, as a referee for that journal, to answer:
 1. Is the mathematics correct? Name any step he cannot follow or believes wrong.
 2. Is it new, and is the priority narrative accurate? Name the nearest prior work and say whether we have credited it correctly. THIS MATTERS: a referee on a sibling paper of ours just found an omitted prior result that changed that paper's whole priority claim, so treat this question as the most important of the four.
 3. Is the increment sufficient for this journal, and if not, which journal fits?
 4. What would he require in revision?

Tell him a hard rejection with reasons is more useful to us than a soft acceptance, and that every objection should come with what would resolve it.

+尊敬的教授：
+
+我们想请您以外部审稿人的标准，专门审查修订稿中新加入的“平稳碰撞实验”定理，而不是继续替我们寻找选题。背景需要如实说明：您此前建议拒绝本文在 Journal of Physics Communications 的投稿，理由包括优先权叙述有误（遗漏 Bickel--Kwon）、若干证明存在缺口，以及 sampled-counter 的物理内容只构成模型解释、并非独立的物理结果。我们随后修复了这些问题，并将论文改投 Methodology and Computing in Applied Probability。后来我们问什么结果才可能真正提高论文层级；您的回答是，数学中心必须离开受约束的二状态模型，因为继续研究二状态 quotient 的空间已受到 Mészáros--Telek 对整个二阶 DRAP/DMAP 类的 canonical representation，以及 Ramírez-Cobo、Lillo 与 Wiper 关于 MAP2 非可识别性和弱等价刻画的限制。您把下述路线评为成功概率 60% × 层级影响 9.0/10，并认为这是唯一现实的整级提升目标。该路线现已完成。
+
+新主节题为“Stationary collision experiments for sampled serial phase-type renewals”，其 headline 结果是“Stationary double-pole experiment for sampled generalized-Erlang renewals”。模型如下。固定阶数 n≥2、已知采样间隔 Δτ>0，并令串联生成元
+
+Q(θ)_{ii}=-θ_i，qquad Q(θ)_{i,i-1}=θ_i（2≤i≤n），
+K_θ=exp(Δτ Q(θ))，qquad T_{1,θ}=(I-K_θ)1e_n^T。
+
+P_θ^{(N)} 是由 killed-reset kernels (K_θ,T_{1,θ}) 生成的长度 N 平稳二元记录的分布；它是对 generalized-Erlang 吸收时间 W=X_1+⋯+X_n 以 Δτ 采样所得的平稳 renewal record。若 θ_1、θ_2 构成唯一的碰撞对，采用 quotient 坐标
+
+c=(θ_1+θ_2)/2，qquad δ=((θ_1-θ_2)/2)^2≥0，qquad η=(c,θ_3,…,θ_n)。
+
+在 compact double-rate stratum C_n(ε,M,c_0) 上，所有 rate 位于 [ε,M]；碰撞率为 (λ,λ)，每个其余 rate 与 λ 至少相隔 c_0，且任意两个其余 rate 也至少相隔 c_0。Palm gap tail、mass 和平均 cycle length 分别记为 S_{η,δ}(k)、g_{η,δ}(k)=S_{η,δ}(k)-S_{η,δ}(k+1) 和 μ_{η,δ}=Σ_{k≥0}S_{η,δ}(k)。在基点 (η_0,0)，令
+
+I_n(η_0)=μ_{η_0,0}^{-1} Σ_{k≥0} g_{η_0,0}(k) · [∇_{(η,δ)}log g_{η,δ}(k)]_0[∇_{(η,δ)}log g_{η,δ}(k)]_0^T。
+
+定理首先给出完整的平稳 LAN / singular limit experiment。对有界 u∈R^{n-1} 和 v∈[0,∞)，在整个 compact stratum 上一致地，
+
+log{dP_{η_0+u/√N,,v/√N}^{(N)}/dP_{η_0,0}^{(N)}}
+=(u,v)^TΔ_{N,η_0}-(1/2)(u,v)^TI_n(η_0)(u,v)+o_{P_{η_0,0}}(1)，
+
+且 Δ_{N,η_0}⇒N_n(0,I_n(η_0))，其 bounded-Lipschitz convergence 以及余项均为 compact-uniform。因此局部平稳记录实验收敛到半空间 R^{n-1}×[0,∞) 上的 Gaussian shift；信息矩阵满足
+
+inf_{η_0∈C_n(ε,M,c_0)} λ_min{I_n(η_0)}>0。
+
+把 I_n 按 (η,δ) 分块并令 J_n=I_{δδ}-I_{δη}I_{ηη}^{-1}I_{ηδ}>0，则 residualized collision-score test（等价地，局部 likelihood-ratio test）对 H_0:δ=0 是一致渐近 level α，并在 δ_N=v/√N 下达到 nuisance-adjusted Gaussian half-plane power envelope
+
+1-Φ_N{z_{1-α}-v√J_n(η_0)}。
+
+估计结论是一个真正由上下界匹配的局部 minimax 结论。用前 2n 个 empirical complete-gap tails 解 confluent recurrence，可先以 root-N 精度估计 smooth quotient coordinates (η,δ)，将 δ 的估计投影到 [0,∞)，再由 c±√δ 恢复碰撞 rate pair。于是 unordered rate multiset 的 optimal-matching error 为 O_P(N^{-1/4})，而 centre 和所有未碰撞 rates 仍为 O_P(N^{-1/2})。更明确地，在 stratum 的任一固定点，固定其余 rates，令
+
+R_N(v)={{c+√(v/√N),c-√(v/√N),θ_3,…,θ_n}}。
+
+对每个 v_0>0 和每个 0<a<√v_0/2，定理的两点下界是
+
+liminf_{N→∞} inf_{\widehat R_N} max_{v∈{0,v_0}}
+P_{η_0,v/√N}^{(N)}{N^{1/4}d_m(\widehat R_N,R_N(v))>a}>0。
+
+因此不存在在这组有界碰撞 alternatives 上一致达到 o_P(N^{-1/4}) 的估计量，而上述 recurrence estimator 达到 O_P(N^{-1/4})；“matching local minimax rate”在文中明确指这两个方向同时成立。若写 θ_1=c+d、θ_2=c-d，则记录只识别 δ=d^2，d 的符号不可识别。
+
+这里 N^{-1/4} 的来源不是此前的 stopped batch-means 论证。它来自对 squared separation δ 的 root-N 估计与 singular square-root map δ↦√δ 的复合。旧结果中的 N^{-1/3} 仍只是选择 b_N≈N^{1/3} 时，带 random stopping、separated batches 和 plug-in centering 的 covariance/bandwidth 估计问题的速率；修订稿没有把该 N^{-1/3} 重新包装成碰撞恢复速率。
+
+证明 machinery 也已实质替换。第一，信息非退化不再依靠旧的 covariance nondegeneracy，而由 sampled pole-order nondegeneracy 给出。连续 survival transform 为
+
+H_{η,δ}(s)=s^{-1}{1-[(c^2-δ)/((s+c)^2-δ)]B_η(s)}，qquad B_η(s)=∏_{j=3}^n θ_j/(s+θ_j)。
+
+在 c=λ、δ=0 处，collision coordinate 的 derivative 有 -λ 处的 exact fourth-order pole，采样后含有其他 score 无法产生的非零 k^3e^{-λΔτ k} 项；centre score 在该 exponential base 上最高达到 degree two；每个 separated-rate score 则在自己的 base e^{-θ_jΔτ} 上含有唯一的 k e^{-θ_jΔτ k} 项。不同 exponential-polynomial bases 及其最高次数的线性独立性依次迫使所有线性组合系数为零，再由 compactness 得到 uniform positive information bound。
+
+第二，证明不再假设确定数目的独立 Palm gaps。若观测窗内 clicks 为 C_1<⋯<C_J，A=C_1、R=N-1-C_J，complete gaps 为 G_1,…,G_{J-1}，则使用 exact stationary likelihood
+
+P_q^{(N)}(record)=μ_q^{-1}S_q(A)[∏_{j=1}^{J-1}g_q(G_j)]S_q(R)。
+
+随机的 J 给出 calendar information 中的 1/μ_q 因子；初始 age、末端 residual survival factors 和 μ_q^{-1} 在 root-N local log likelihood 中一致为 o_P(1)。证明随后对随机停止的 complete-gap score 作 Taylor expansion 和 stopped i.i.d. CLT，并用共同的 exponential/polynomial envelope 控制三阶余项，而不是沿用 deterministic Palm-product split。
+
+第三，上界使用新的 sampled-tail recurrence estimator：leading Hankel matrix 在 double pole 处仍非奇异，recurrence coefficients、simple roots 以及 double cluster 的 elementary symmetric functions 均为 root-N；squared arcosh 的 analytic continuation 给出 δ 的 root-N estimator，随后 square root 产生 N^{-1/4}。下界则比较 v=0 与 v=v_0；两组 rate multisets 恰相隔 √v_0N^{-1/4}，LAN likelihood ratio 收敛到有限且非退化的 Gaussian likelihood ratio，从而两列实验 mutually contiguous。若某估计量在两点都达到 o_P(N^{-1/4})，按距离较近的 candidate 分类便会一致区分这两列 contiguous experiments，产生矛盾。
+
+范围限制完全按正文保留：只允许 one double collision；所有其他 rates 均 simple 且 uniformly separated；不声称覆盖 simultaneous collisions、unknown order、unknown sampling interval 或 nonserial phase-type representations。这正是您为 60% 成功率估计所附的条件；我们采用了这些条件，没有追求您当时未评估成本的额外一般性。
+
+这些结论及其 uniform remainder arguments 都是解析证明。数值 batteries 仅作 sanity checks，未被用作定理正确性或非退化性的证据。我们特别遵守您先前的警告：对 LAN、Gaussian approximation 等 infinite-sample theorem，numerics 永远不能替代 uniform remainder argument。
+
+请您现在以严格 referee 标准回答以下问题：
+
+1. 证明是否正确？请点名任何您无法跟随、认为错误或认为缺少 uniform justification 的步骤，尤其审查 sampled pole-order nondegeneracy、带 random stopping 与 endpoint factors 的 exact stationary likelihood，以及 contiguity lower bound。对每个问题，请说明需要补充哪条 lemma、bound、引用、反例排除或改写才能解决。
+
+2. minimax 措辞是否诚实？上述两点下界是否真的与 recurrence estimator 的 O_P(N^{-1/4}) 上界构成 N^{-1/4} 的 matching local minimax bound，还是它实际只证明了比“matching”更弱的结论？若更弱，请给出准确可成立的表述以及达到 matching 所欠缺的量化条件。
+
+3. 这是否在您原先要求的意义上真正把论文数学中心移出了二状态模型，还是仍只是一个披着 phase-type 外衣的 constrained special case？请区分“固定 serial/generalized-Erlang family 是有意义的标准对象”与“只是把 bespoke two-state calculation 机械地写成 n-state notation”这两种判断，并说明什么证据或扩展会改变您的结论。
+
+4. 结果是否新颖？我们七篇论文中已有六篇被发现存在 priority omissions，因此请把这一项当作关键审查：在 singular-experiment、phase-type、generalized-Erlang/Coxian、sampled renewal、loss-of-identifiability 或相关文献中，是否已经存在针对 sampled phase-type renewals 的 stationary double-pole LAN，以及 colliding rates 的 matching N^{-1/4} recovery theorem？请点名最接近的先行结果，逐项判断本定理是直接推论、已知框架的非平凡 specialization，还是新的结果，并指出必须增加或修正的引用与 priority language。
+
+5. 加入该结果后，论文现在诚实的层级与 venue 判断是什么？Methodology and Computing in Applied Probability 是否合适；该 theorem 是否达到了您原先所说的“整级提升”，还是正确但不足以改变论文定位？若不适合，请给出具体期刊和理由，并分别说明限制来自 novelty、depth、generality、article architecture，还是与 sampled-counter physics 的联系仍仅为模型解释。
+
+有充分理由的 hard rejection 对我们比含糊的 soft acceptance 更有价值。请让每一项反对意见都同时附带明确的 resolution condition：什么证明、文献核查、范围收缩、措辞降级、重组或新增结果足以解除该异议。
